3:T1a94,---
title: ProFields
description: Standalone field primitives for forms — Input, Password, Textarea, Digit, Select, DatePicker, Upload, Captcha, ArrayField, and more.
---

## Installation

<CodeTabs>

<TabsList>
  <TabsTrigger value="cli">CLI</TabsTrigger>
  <TabsTrigger value="manual">Manual</TabsTrigger>
</TabsList>

<TabsContent value="cli">

```bash
npx @draco-china/shadcn-pro@latest add pro-fields
```

</TabsContent>

<TabsContent value="manual">

<Steps className="mb-0 pt-2">

<Step>Install the following dependencies:</Step>

```bash
bun add @dnd-kit/core @dnd-kit/sortable @dnd-kit/utilities class-variance-authority cmdk date-fns lucide-react radix-ui react-day-picker
```

<Step>Install or copy `pro-base`.</Step>

<Step>
  Copy and paste the component files into your project under
  `components/pro/base/fields/`.
</Step>

Manual installs include these field files:

- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/field.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/shared/calendar.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/input/index.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/select/index.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/checkbox/index.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/radio/index.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/date-picker/index.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/date-time-picker/index.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/upload/index.tsx`
- `components/pro/base/fields/array-field/index.tsx`

<Step>Update the import paths to match your project setup.</Step>

</Steps>

</TabsContent>

</CodeTabs>

## Usage

Every field is a plain React/shadcn component. Simple fields preserve the underlying shadcn/ui prop surface where practical; shadcn-pro adds only small behaviors such as clear actions, affix slots, and options mapping. Compose section layouts at the call site.

`Input` only applies the default horizontal padding when no affix is present. When `prefix` or `suffix` is provided, the shell keeps the slot flush and the passed content owns its own spacing, such as `px-3` on a currency prefix.

### Select

<ComponentPreview
  name="pro-fields-select-demo"
  previewClassName="min-h-[160px] items-start p-6"
  align="start"
/>


Some fields expose `allowClear` when clearing is an optional behavior. Digit, DigitRange, date, time, Cascader, and TreeSelect fields keep clearing built in: when they have a value, the clear button is shown automatically.

`Select`, `Radio`, and grouped `Checkbox` options support `description` for consistent option help text. `Select` also supports `searchable` and `multiple`; multiple mode uses a `string[]` value.


### Checkbox

<ComponentPreview
  name="pro-fields-checkbox-demo"
  previewClassName="min-h-[140px] items-start p-6"
  align="start"
/>


Use `options` for checkbox group mode. In group mode the value is `string[]`; without `options`, Checkbox uses boolean switch semantics.

### Upload

<ComponentPreview
  name="pro-fields-upload-demo"
  previewClassName="min-h-[240px] items-start p-6"
  align="start"
/>


`UploadTrigger` supports `dropzone` and `compact` variants. Compose it with `UploadFileList` inside the root, and keep custom URL inputs outside with your own controlled `Input` state.


For single-file URL fields, store the pasted URL as an `UploadFile[]` yourself and pass the same value to `Upload` when you also want local file selection.

### ArrayField

Drag-to-reorder list with built-in state management. Supports controlled (`value` + `onChange`) and uncontrolled (`defaultValue`) usage.

<ComponentPreview
  name="pro-fields-array-field-demo"
  previewClassName="min-h-[280px] items-start p-6"
  align="start"
/>

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
|------|------|---------|-------------|
| `value` | `TItem[]` | — | Controlled array value |
| `onChange` | `(value: TItem[]) => void` | — | Called on any change |
| `defaultValue` | `TItem[]` | `[]` | Initial value (uncontrolled) |
| `newItem` | `() => TItem` | — | Factory for blank items |
| `renderItem` | `(item, index, helpers) => ReactNode` | — | Render each item. `helpers.update` accepts a replacement value or updater function; `helpers.duplicate` copies the row; `helpers.remove` removes it. |
| `max` | `number` | — | Hide Add button when reached |
| `min` | `number` | `0` | Hide Remove button when at minimum |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables all controls |

77:T663,"use client"

import { useState } from "react"

import { Select } from "@/components/pro/base/fields/select"

export function ProFieldsSelectDemo() {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<string | undefined>("admin")
  const [roles, setRoles] = useState<string[]>(["editor"])

  return (
    <div className="grid w-full max-w-sm gap-4 p-4">
      <Select
        value={value}
        onChange={(next) => setValue(typeof next === "string" ? next : undefined)}
        allowClear
        searchable
        options={[
          {
            label: "Admin",
            value: "admin",
            description: "Full access to workspace settings.",
          },
          {
            label: "Editor",
            value: "editor",
            description: "Can create and update content.",
          },
          {
            label: "Viewer",
            value: "viewer",
            description: "Read-only project access.",
          },
        ]}
      />
      <Select
        value={roles}
        onChange={(next) => setRoles(Array.isArray(next) ? next : [])}
        multiple
        searchable
        allowClear
        placeholder="Select roles"
        options={[
          {
            label: "Admin",
            value: "admin",
            description: "Full access to workspace settings.",
          },
          {
            label: "Editor",
            value: "editor",
            description: "Can create and update content.",
          },
          {
            label: "Viewer",
            value: "viewer",
            description: "Read-only project access.",
          },
        ]}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

a5:T469,"use client"

import { useState } from "react"

import { ArrayField } from "@/components/pro/base/fields/array-field"
import { Input } from "@/components/pro/base/fields/input"

interface Contact {
  name: string
  email: string
}

export function ProFieldsArrayFieldDemo() {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<Contact[]>([
    { name: "Alice Wang", email: "alice@example.com" },
    { name: "Bob Chen", email: "bob@example.com" },
  ])

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md p-4">
      <ArrayField<Contact>
        value={value}
        onChange={setValue}
        newItem={() => ({ name: "", email: "" })}
        min={1}
        renderItem={(item, _index, { update }) => (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Input
              placeholder="Name"
              value={item.name}
              onChange={(e) => update({ ...item, name: e.target.value })}
            />
            <Input
              placeholder="Email"
              value={item.email}
              onChange={(e) => update({ ...item, email: e.target.value })}
            />
          </div>
        )}
      />
    </div>
  )
}
